\shneg{\mathbf{P}}=F^{\perp\perp}
. From (
2
) we deduce
F^{\perp\perp}\subseteq E^{\perp}
, and from (
1
)
E^{\perp}=(E\cup\{\maltese\})^{\perp}\subseteq F^{\perp\perp}
. Hence
\shneg{\mathbf{P}}=F^{\perp\perp}=E^{\perp}=(\shpos{\mathbf{P}}^{\perp})^{\perp}
.
∎

Proposition 78
.

1.

V_{{\mathbf{\shpos N}}}=\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\}

2.

V_{{\mathbf{\shneg P}}}=\kappa_{\blacktriangle}V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{x}\cup\{\epsilon\}

Proof.

1.

(\subseteq)
Let
{\mathfrak{q}}\in\shpos{\mathbf{N}}
and
{\mathfrak{m}}\in(\shpos{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
, let us show that
\langle{\mathfrak{q}}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{m}}\rangle\in\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\}
. By Lemma
77
,
{\mathfrak{m}}\in\shneg{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}
. If
{\mathfrak{q}}=\maltese
then
\langle{\mathfrak{q}}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{m}}\rangle=\maltese
. Otherwise, by Theorem
8
,
{\mathfrak{q}}=\blacktriangledown\langle{\mathfrak{n}}\rangle
with
{\mathfrak{n}}\in{\mathbf{N}}
. We have
\langle{\mathfrak{q}}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{m}}\rangle=\langle{\mathfrak{q}}\leftarrow|{\mathfrak{m}}|\rangle
by Lemma
70
, where
|{\mathfrak{m}}|\in\blacktriangle(x).|({\mathbf{N}}^{\perp})^{x}|
by Theorem
8
, hence
|{\mathfrak{m}}|
is of the form
|{\mathfrak{m}}|=\blacktriangle(x).{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}
with
{\mathfrak{p}}\in{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}
. By definition
\langle{\mathfrak{q}}\leftarrow|{\mathfrak{m}}|\rangle=\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}\langle{\mathfrak{n}}^{x}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\rangle
, where
\langle{\mathfrak{n}}^{x}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\rangle\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}
.

(\supseteq)
Indeed
\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{\shpos N}}}
. Now let
\mathpzc{s}\in\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}
. There exist
{\mathfrak{n}}\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}\in{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}
such that
\mathpzc{s}=\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}\langle{\mathfrak{n}}^{x}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\rangle
. Note that
\blacktriangledown\langle{\mathfrak{n}}\rangle\in\blacktriangledown\langle{\mathbf{N}}\rangle
and
\blacktriangle(x).{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\in\blacktriangle(x).({\mathbf{N}}^{\perp})^{x}
. By Lemma
77
,
\shneg{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}=(\shpos{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
, hence
\blacktriangledown\langle{\mathfrak{n}}\rangle\perp\blacktriangle(x).{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}
. Moreover
\langle\blacktriangledown\langle{\mathfrak{n}}\rangle\leftarrow\blacktriangle(x).{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\rangle=\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}\langle{\mathfrak{n}}^{x}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{p}}^{x}\rangle=\mathpzc{s}
, therefore
\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{\shpos N}}}
.

2.

By Lemma
77
and previous item, and remarking that
V_{{\mathbf{B}}}=\makebox[19.20415pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1258563}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{B^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1258563}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{B^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1258563}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{B^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1258563}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{B^{\perp}}}}}}$}}
for every behaviour
{\mathbf{B}}
, we have:

V_{{\mathbf{\shneg P}}}=\makebox[30.45558pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1995937}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{(\shneg P)^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1995937}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{(\shneg P)^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1995937}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{(\shneg P)^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1995937}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{(\shneg P)^{\perp}}}}}}$}}=\makebox[24.20555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1586335}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{\shpos P^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1586335}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{\shpos P^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1586335}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{\shpos P^{\perp}}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1586335}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{\shpos P^{\perp}}}}}}$}}=\makebox[63.3978pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.5pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{4154838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{(\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{4154838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{(\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{4154838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{(\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{4154838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{(\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}\cup\{\maltese\})}}$}}=\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\makebox[18.95555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1242271}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1242271}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1242271}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{1242271}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P^{\perp}}}}^{x}}}$}}\cup\{\epsilon\}=\kappa_{\blacktriangle}V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{x}\cup\{\epsilon\}
.

∎

B.2.2
Plus

Proposition 79
.

V_{{\mathbf{M\oplus N}}}=\kappa_{\iota_{1}}V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x_{1}}\cup\kappa_{\iota_{2}}V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x_{2}}\cup\{\maltese\}

Proof.

Remark that
{\mathbf{M}}\oplus{\mathbf{N}}=(\iota_{1}\langle{\mathbf{M}}\rangle\cup\{\maltese\})\cup(\iota_{2}\langle{\mathbf{N}}\rangle\cup\{\maltese\})
is the union of behaviours
\oplus_{1}{\mathbf{M}}
and
\oplus_{2}{\mathbf{N}}
, which correspond respectively to
\shpos{\mathbf{M}}
and
\shpos{\mathbf{N}}
with a different name for the first action. Moreover,
({\mathbf{M}}\oplus{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}=\{{\mathfrak{n}}\penalty\ \boldsymbol{|}\penalty\ \mathinner{\mathchoice{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{1}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{1}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\pi_{1}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{1}}}\in\pi_{1}(x).({\mathbf{M}}^{\perp})^{x}\mbox{ and }\mathinner{\mathchoice{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{2}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{2}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\pi_{2}}{{\mathfrak{n}}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\pi_{2}}}\in\pi_{2}(x).({\mathbf{N}}^{\perp})^{x}\}=(\with_{1}{\mathbf{M}}^{\perp})\cap(\with_{2}{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp})
, where the behaviours
\with_{1}{\mathbf{M}}^{\perp}
and
\with_{2}{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}
correspond to
\shneg{\mathbf{M}}^{\perp}
and
\shneg{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}
with different names; note also that for every
{\mathfrak{d}}\in|\with_{1}{\mathbf{M}}^{\perp}|
(resp.
|\with_{2}{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}|
) there exists
{\mathfrak{d}}^{\prime}\in({\mathbf{M}}\oplus{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
such that
{\mathfrak{d}}\sqsubseteq{\mathfrak{d}}^{\prime}
, in other words such that
{\mathfrak{d}}=|{\mathfrak{d}}^{\prime}|_{\with_{1}{\mathbf{M}}^{\perp}}
(resp.
|{\mathfrak{d}}^{\prime}|_{\with_{2}{\mathbf{N}}^{\perp}}
). Therefore the proof can be conducted similarly to the one of Proposition
78
(1).
∎

B.2.3
Tensor and Linear Map

The following proposition is a joint work with Fouqueré and Quatrini; in
[
9
]
, they prove a similar result in the framework of original Ludics.

Proposition 80
.

\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{M\otimes N}}}
if and only if the two conditions below are satisfied:

1.

\mathpzc{s}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})\cup\{\maltese\}
,

2.

for all
\mathpzc{t}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
, for all
\kappa^{-}
such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
,
\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
.

The proof of this proposition uses some material on multi-designs introduced in Section
A
. Note also that for all negative designs
{\mathfrak{m}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}
, we will write
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
instead of
x_{0}|\bullet\langle{\mathfrak{m}},{\mathfrak{n}}\rangle
.

Proof.

(\Rightarrow)
Let
\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{M\otimes N}}}
. If
\mathpzc{s}=\maltese
then both conditions are trivial, so suppose
\mathpzc{s}\neq\maltese
. By internal completeness (Theorem
8
), there exist
{\mathfrak{m}}\in{\mathbf{M}}
,
{\mathfrak{n}}\in{\mathbf{N}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
such that
\mathpzc{s}=\langle{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}\rangle
. Thus
{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}
must be of the form
{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}=\sum_{a\in\mathcal{S}}a(\overrightarrow{z^{a}}).{\mathfrak{p}}_{a}
with
{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}\neq\Omega
(remember that
\bullet=\overline{\wp}
), and we have
\mathpzc{s}=\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
where
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}=\langle\{{\mathfrak{m}}^{x},{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}\}\leftarrow\{{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp},{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}/x_{0}\}\rangle=\langle\{{\mathfrak{m}}^{x},{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}\}\leftarrow{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}\rangle
. Let us prove both properties:

1.

By Proposition
66
,
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}}=\langle{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}\leftarrow(\![{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)\rangle
, where
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x}
. Moreover,
(\![{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x\perp}
, indeed: for any
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{M}}
, we have
(\![(\![{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)[{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}/x]]\!)=(\![{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y,{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}/x]]\!)=(\![({\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}})[{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}/x_{0}]]\!)=\maltese
using associativity and one reduction step backwards. Thus
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
. Likewise,
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}}=\langle{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}\leftarrow(\![{\mathfrak{p}}_{\wp}[{\mathfrak{m}}/x]]\!)\rangle
, so
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
. Therefore
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\in(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})
.

2.

Now let
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x},\mathpzc{t}_{2}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
. Suppose
\mathpzc{t}\in(\mathpzc{t}_{1}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}_{2})
and
\kappa^{-}
is a negative action such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Without loss of generality, suppose moreover that the action
\kappa^{-}
comes from
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}
, and let us show that
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
.

Let
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}=\langle\{\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/x,\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{2}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/y\}\leftarrow\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\rangle
. We will show that
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}
is a prefix of
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}
and that
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
, leading to the conclusion by Lemma
71
. Note the following facts:

(a)

\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}=\wp(x,y).\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}+\sum_{a\neq\wp}a(\overrightarrow{z^{a}}).\maltese
, and thus
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\neq\maltese
(otherwise a path of the form
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
cannot be path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
).

(b)

\mathpzc{t}
is a path of the multi-design
\{\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/x,\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{2}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/y\}
, and
\overline{\mathpzc{t}}
is a prefix of a path of
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
since
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, thus
\mathpzc{t}
is a prefix of
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
by Proposition
62
.

(c)

Since
\mathpzc{t}
is a
\maltese
-free positive-ended prefix of
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
, we have that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}}
is a strict prefix of
\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}
. Thus there exists a positive action
\kappa_{0}^{+}
such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}}\kappa_{0}^{+}
is a prefix of
\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{1117603}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}
. The paths
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
and
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}}\kappa^{+}_{0}
are both paths of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, hence necessarily
\kappa^{+}_{0}=\overline{\kappa^{-}}
. We deduce that
\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}
is a prefix of
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
.

(d)

The sequence
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}
therefore starts with
\mathinner{\mathchoice{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}
.

(e)

We have
\mathinner{\mathchoice{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}=(\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}})\kappa^{-}
because, since
\kappa^{-}
comes from
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}
, it is hereditarily justified by an initial negative action of address
x
, and thus
\kappa^{-}
appears in design
\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. We deduce
\mathinner{\mathchoice{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{(\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-})\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}=(\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}})\kappa^{-}=\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}
.

(f)

Moreover, by Proposition
66

\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}=\langle\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\leftarrow(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{2}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/y]]\!)\rangle
.

Hence (by d, e, f) the sequence
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}
is a prefix of
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}=\langle\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\leftarrow(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{2}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/y]]\!)\rangle
. Since
\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x}
(by Proposition
69
) and
(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{2}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/y]]\!)\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x\perp}
(by associativity, similar reasoning as item
1
), we deduce
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}}\raisebox{1.2284pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}}}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
. Finally
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
by Lemma
71
.

(\Leftarrow)
Let
\mathpzc{s}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})\cup\{\maltese\}
such that the second constraint is also satisfied. If
\mathpzc{s}=\maltese
then
\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{M\otimes N}}}
is immediate, so suppose
\mathpzc{s}=\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
where
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\in(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})
. Consider the design
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, and note that
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}=\wp(x,y).\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}+\sum_{a\neq\wp}a(\overrightarrow{z^{a}}).\maltese
. We will show by contradiction that
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
, leading to the conclusion.

Let
{\mathfrak{m}}\in{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}\in{\mathbf{N}}
such that
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}\not\perp\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. By Proposition
64
and given the form of design
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, the interaction with
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
is finite and the cause of divergence is necessarily the existence of a path
\mathpzc{t}
and an action
\kappa^{-}
such that:

1.

\mathpzc{t}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
,

2.

\overline{\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}

3.

\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}
is not a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
.

Hence
\mathpzc{t}
is of the form
\mathpzc{t}=\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
. Choose
{\mathfrak{m}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}
such that
\mathpzc{t}
is of minimal length with respect to all such pairs of designs non orthogonal to
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Let
\mathpzc{t}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}}
and
\mathpzc{t}_{2}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}}}
, we have
\mathpzc{t}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(\mathpzc{t}_{1}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}_{2})
. Consider the design
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, and note that
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}=\wp(x,y).\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}+\sum_{a\neq\wp}a(\overrightarrow{z^{a}}).\maltese
. We prove the following:

•

\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
: By contradiction. Let
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{N}}
such that
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}^{\prime}\not\perp\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Again using Proposition
64
, divergence occurs necessarily because there exists a path
\mathpzc{v}
and a negative action
\kappa^{\prime-}
such that:

1.

\mathpzc{v}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}^{\prime}
,

2.

\overline{\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
,

3.

\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}
is not a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}^{\prime}
.

Since the views of
\overline{\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}}
are views of
\overline{\mathpzc{t}}
,
\overline{\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Thus
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}^{\prime}\not\perp\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Moreover
\mathpzc{v}
is strictly shorter than
\mathpzc{t}
, indeed:
\mathpzc{v}
and
\mathpzc{t}
are
\maltese
-free, and since
\overline{\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
any action of
\mathpzc{v}\kappa^{\prime-}
is an action of
\mathpzc{t}
. This contradicts the fact that
\mathpzc{t}
is of minimum length. We deduce
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
.

•

\mathpzc{t}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})
: We show
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
, the proof of
\mathpzc{t}_{2}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
being similar. Since
\mathpzc{t}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
and
\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}
a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, we have
\mathpzc{t}=\langle{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}\leftarrow\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\rangle=\kappa_{\bullet}\langle\{{\mathfrak{m}}^{x},{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}\}\leftarrow\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\rangle
, hence
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}=\langle\{{\mathfrak{m}}^{x},{\mathfrak{n}}^{y}\}\leftarrow\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\rangle
. Thus by Proposition
66

\mathpzc{t}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}}}=\langle{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}\leftarrow(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)\rangle
. Moreover
(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x\perp}
: for any design
{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\in{\mathbf{M}}
we have
(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)\perp{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime x}
because of the equality
(\![(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y]]\!)[{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}/x]]\!)=(\![\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.69446pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{438728}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}[{\mathfrak{n}}/y,{\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}/x]]\!)=(\![({\mathfrak{m}}^{\prime}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}})[\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}/x_{0}]]\!)=\maltese
, using associativity, one reduction step backwards, and the fact that
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.8889pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.15079pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.15079pt}{$\resizebox{254863}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{t}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
. It follows that
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
.

•

\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
: Remember that
\overline{\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, and we have just seen that
\mathpzc{t}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})
. Using the second constraint of the proposition, we should have
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
or
\mathpzc{t}_{2}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
. Without loss of generality suppose
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
. Since
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}\in{\mathbf{M}}^{x}
and
\mathpzc{t}_{1}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}
, we should also have that
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}
is a prefix of a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}
by Lemma
72
, hence
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\kappa^{-}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\urcorner$}=\raisebox{1.72041pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}}\raisebox{1.72041pt}{$\urcorner$}
is a view of
{\mathfrak{m}}^{x}
. But in this case, knowing that
\mathpzc{t}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
and that
\raisebox{1.72041pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}\raisebox{1.72041pt}{$\urcorner$}=\kappa_{\bullet}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}\kappa^{-}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\urcorner$}
is a view of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
, we deduce that
\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
.

Last point contradicts the cause of divergence between
{\mathfrak{m}}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}
and
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Hence
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\in({\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}})^{\perp}
. Moreover,
\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, and since
\mathpzc{s}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})
there exist
{\mathfrak{m}}_{0}\in{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}\in{\mathbf{N}}
such that
\mathpzc{s}
is a path of
{\mathfrak{m}}_{0}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}
(and
{\mathfrak{m}}_{0}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}\in{\mathbf{M}}\otimes{\mathbf{N}}
). We deduce
\mathpzc{s}=\langle{\mathfrak{m}}_{0}\otimes{\mathfrak{n}}_{0}\leftarrow\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}\rangle
, hence
\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{M\otimes N}}}
.
∎

Corollary 81
.

\mathpzc{s}\in V_{{\mathbf{N\multimap P}}}
if and only if the two conditions below are satisfied:

1.

\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}$}})\cup\{\maltese\}

2.

for all
\mathpzc{t}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}$}}
, for all
\kappa^{-}
such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{-0.61685pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\mathpzc{s}}\raisebox{-0.61685pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
,
\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}^{y}}}$}}
.

B.2.4
Tensor and Linear Map, Regular Case

Proposition 82
.

If
{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathbf{N}}
regular then
V_{{\mathbf{M\otimes N}}}=\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})\cup\{\maltese\}
.

Proof.

Suppose
{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathbf{N}}
regular. Following Proposition
80
, it suffices to show that any path
\mathpzc{s}\in\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y})\cup\{\maltese\}
satisfies the following condition: for all
\mathpzc{t}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
, for all negative action
\kappa^{-}
such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
,
\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
.

If
\mathpzc{s}=\maltese
, there is nothing to prove, so suppose
\mathpzc{s}=\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
where
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
. Let
\mathpzc{t}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}\shuffle V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
and
\kappa^{-}
be such that
\overline{\kappa_{\bullet}\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[3.94444pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{4.30554pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{4.30554pt}{$\resizebox{258503}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}}}$}}}\raisebox{1.52205pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
, that is
\overline{\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
. Let
\mathpzc{s}_{1},\mathpzc{t}_{1}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
and
\mathpzc{s}_{2},\mathpzc{t}_{2}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{y}
such that
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\in\mathpzc{s}_{1}\shuffle\mathpzc{s}_{2}
and
\mathpzc{t}\in\mathpzc{t}_{1}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}_{2}
. Without loss of generality, suppose
\kappa^{-}
is an action in
\mathpzc{s}_{1}
, thus we must show
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
. Notice that
\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\rangle=\langle\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}\rangle=\langle\kappa^{-}\rangle_{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}=\langle\kappa^{-}\rangle_{\mathpzc{s}_{1}}
(the second equality follows from the fact that
\overline{\mathpzc{t}\kappa^{-}}
is a path of
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner\mkern-6.0mu\ulcorner\mkern-2.0mu$}{\,\makebox[6.75pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.51782pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.51782pt}{$\resizebox{442368}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}}}$}}\,}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\mkern-2.0mu\urcorner\mkern-6.0mu\urcorner$}^{c}
). Since
\mathpzc{s}_{1}\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
, the sequence
\langle\kappa^{-}\rangle_{\mathpzc{s}_{1}}=\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\rangle
is a trivial view of
{\mathbf{M}}^{x}
. Let
\mathpzc{s}_{1}^{\prime}\kappa^{-}
be the prefix of
\mathpzc{s}_{1}
ending with
\kappa^{-}
. By Lemma
71

\mathpzc{s}_{1}^{\prime}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
, so
\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\rangle=\langle\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\rangle
is also a trivial view of
{\mathbf{M}}^{x}
; by regularity of
{\mathbf{M}}
, we deduce
\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\rangle\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
. We have
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in\mathpzc{t}_{1}\shuffle\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\rangle
, where both
\mathpzc{t}_{1}
and
\langle\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\rangle
are in
V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
, hence
\mathpzc{t}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese\in V_{{\mathbf{M}}}^{x}
by regularity of
{\mathbf{M}}
.
∎

Corollary 83
.

If
{\mathbf{N}}
and
{\mathbf{P}}
are regular then
V_{{\mathbf{N\multimap P}}}=\makebox[56.74496pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{7.5pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{3718838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{N}}}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}$}})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{3718838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{N}}}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}$}})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{3718838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{N}}}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}$}})}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{7.5pt}{$\resizebox{3718838}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{\kappa_{\bullet}(V_{{\mathbf{N}}}\shuffle\makebox[14.70555pt][c]{\mbox{\rule{0.0pt}{6.83331pt}$\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{6.83331pt}{$\resizebox{963743}{0}{$\sim$}$}\hss}{V_{{\mathbf{P}}}}}$}})}}$}}\cup\{\epsilon\}
.

B.3
Proof of Proposition
18
: Regularity and Connectives

Proposition 84
.

1.

If
{\mathbf{N}}
is regular then
\shpos{\mathbf{N}}
is regular.

2.

If
{\mathbf{P}}
is regular then
\shneg{\mathbf{P}}
is regular.

Proof.

1.

Following Proposition
75
:

•

By internal completeness, the trivial views of
\shpos{\mathbf{N}}
are of the form
\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}{\mathbb{t}}
where
{\mathbb{t}}
is a trivial view of
{\mathbf{N}}
. Since
{\mathbf{N}}
is regular
{\mathbb{t}}\in V_{{\mathbf{N}}}
. Hence by Proposition
78
,
\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}{\mathbb{t}}\in V_{{\mathbf{\shpos N}}}
.

•

Since
V_{{\mathbf{N}}}
is stable by shuffle, so is
V_{{\mathbf{\shpos N}}}=\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}V_{{\mathbf{N}}}^{x}
where
\kappa_{\blacktriangledown}
is a positive action.

•

For all paths
\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{s}
,
\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{t}\in V_{{\mathbf{(\shpos N)^{\perp}}}}=\kappa_{\blacktriangle}V_{{\mathbf{N^{\perp}}}}^{x}
such that
\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{t}
is defined,
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
start necessarily by the same positive action and
\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}\subseteq V_{{\mathbf{N^{\perp}}}}^{x}
because
V_{{\mathbf{N^{\perp}}}}
(thus also
V_{{\mathbf{N^{\perp}}}}^{x}
) is stable by
\shuffle
, hence
\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\kappa_{\blacktriangle}\mathpzc{t}=\kappa_{\blacktriangle}(\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\mathpzc{t})\subseteq V_{{\mathbf{(\shpos N)^{\perp}}}}
.

2.

If
{\mathbf{P}}
is regular then
{\mathbf{P}}^{\perp}
is too. Then by previous point
\shpos{\mathbf{P}}^{\perp}
is regular, therefore so is
(\shpos{\mathbf{P}}^{\perp})^{\perp}
. By Lemma
77
, this means that
\shneg{\mathbf{P}}
is regular.

∎

Proposition 85
.

If
{\mathbf{M}}
and
{\mathbf{N}}
are regular then
{\mathbf{M}}\oplus{\mathbf{N}}
is regular.

Proof.

Similar to Proposition
84
(1), by the same remark as in proof of Proposition
79
.
∎

In order to show that
\otimes
preserves regularity, consider first the following definitions and lemma. We call
quasi-path
a positive-ended P-visible aj-sequence. The
shuffle

\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}
of two negative quasi-paths
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
is the set of paths
\mathpzc{u}
formed with actions from
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
such that
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}}=\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}}=\mathpzc{t}
.

Lemma 86
.

Let
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
be negative quasi-paths. If
\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}\neq\emptyset
then
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
are paths.

Proof.

We prove the result by contradiction. Let us suppose that there exists a triple
(\mathpzc{s},\mathpzc{t},\mathpzc{u})
such that
\mathpzc{s}
and
\mathpzc{t}
are two negative quasi-paths,
\mathpzc{u}\in\mathpzc{s}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}
is a path, and at least one of
\mathpzc{s}
or
\mathpzc{t}
does not satisfy O-visibility, say
\mathpzc{s}
: there exists a negative action
\kappa^{-}
and a prefix
\mathpzc{s}_{0}\kappa^{-}
of
\mathpzc{s}
such that the action
\kappa^{-}
is justified in
\mathpzc{s}_{0}
but
\mathrm{just}(\kappa^{-})
does not appear in
{\llcorner\mathpzc{s}_{0}\lrcorner}
.

We choose the triple
(\mathpzc{s},\mathpzc{t},\mathpzc{u})
such that the length of
\mathpzc{u}
is minimal with respect to all such triples. Without loss of generality, we can assume that
\mathpzc{u}
and
\mathpzc{s}
are of the form
\mathpzc{u}=\mathpzc{u}_{0}\kappa^{-}\maltese
and
\mathpzc{s}=\mathpzc{s}_{0}\kappa^{-}\maltese
respectively. Indeed, if this is not true,
\mathpzc{u}
has a strict prefix of the form
\mathpzc{u}_{0}\kappa^{-}
; in this case we can replace
(\mathpzc{s},\mathpzc{t},\mathpzc{u})
by the triple
(\mathpzc{s}_{0}\kappa^{-}\maltese,\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}_{0}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{0}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{0}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{0}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}},\mathpzc{u}_{0}\kappa^{-}\maltese)
which satisfies all the constraints, and where the length of
\mathpzc{u}_{0}\kappa^{-}\maltese
is less or equal to the length of
\mathpzc{u}
.

Let
\kappa^{+}=\mathrm{just}(\kappa^{-})
.
\mathpzc{u}
is necessarily of the form
\mathpzc{u}=\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\kappa^{-}\maltese
where
\alpha^{-}
justifies
\alpha^{+}
and
\kappa^{+}
appears in
\mathpzc{u}_{1}
, indeed:

•

\kappa^{+}
does not appear immediately before
\kappa^{-}
in
\mathpzc{u}
, otherwise it would also be the case in
\mathpzc{s}
, contradicting the fact that
\kappa^{-}
is not O-visible in
\mathpzc{s}
.

•

The action
\alpha^{+}
which is immediately before
\kappa^{-}
in
\mathpzc{u}
is justified by an action
\alpha^{-}
, and
\kappa^{+}
appears before
\alpha^{-}
in
\mathpzc{u}
, otherwise
\kappa^{+}
would not appear in
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{0}\lrcorner}
and that would contradict O-visibility of
\mathpzc{u}
.

Let us show by contradiction something that will be useful for the rest of this proof: in the path
\mathpzc{u}
, all the actions of
\mathpzc{u}_{2}
(which cannot be initial) are justified in
\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}
. If it is not the case, let
\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{2}\beta
be longest prefix of
\mathpzc{u}
such that
\beta
is an action of
\mathpzc{u}_{2}
justified in
\mathpzc{u}_{1}
, and let
\beta^{\prime}
be the following action (necessarily in
\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}
), thus
\beta^{\prime}
is justified in
\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}
. If
\beta^{\prime}
is positive (resp. negative) then
\beta
is negative (resp. positive), thus
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{2}\beta}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\urcorner$}=\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{1}}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\urcorner$}
(resp.
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{2}\beta\lrcorner}={\llcorner\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{1}\lrcorner}
) where
\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{1}
is the prefix of
\mathpzc{u}_{1}
ending on
\mathrm{just}(\beta)
. But then
\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\ulcorner$}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{2}\beta}\raisebox{2.59543pt}{$\urcorner$}
(resp.
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}_{2}\beta\lrcorner}
) does not contain
\mathrm{just}(\beta^{\prime})
: this contradicts the fact that
\mathpzc{u}
is a path, since P-visibility (resp. O-visibility) is not satisfied.

Now define
\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}=\mathpzc{u}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese
,
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}}
and
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}}
, and remark that:

•

\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}
is a path
, indeed, O-visibility for
\kappa^{-}
is still satisfied since
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\kappa^{-}\lrcorner}={\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\lrcorner}\alpha^{-}\alpha^{+}\kappa^{-}
and
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\kappa^{-}\lrcorner}={\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\lrcorner}\kappa^{-}
both contain
\kappa^{+}
in
{\llcorner\mathpzc{u}_{1}\lrcorner}
.

•

\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
and
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
are quasi-paths
, since
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
is of the form
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}=\mathpzc{s}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese
where
\mathpzc{s}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}}
is a prefix of
\mathpzc{s}
containing
\kappa^{+}=\mathrm{just}(\kappa^{-})
, and
\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{t}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{t}}}
is a prefix of
\mathpzc{t}
.

•

\mathpzc{u}^{\prime}\in\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}\shuffle\mathpzc{t}^{\prime}
.

•

\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}
is not a path
: Note that
\mathpzc{s}
is of the form
\mathpzc{s}_{1}\mathpzc{s}_{2}\kappa^{-}\maltese
where
\mathpzc{s}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{s}}{\mathpzc{u}_{1}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}}
and
\mathpzc{s}_{2}=\mathinner{\mathchoice{\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}{\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\mkern 1.0mu\upharpoonright\mkern 1.0mu\mathpzc{s}}{\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}\alpha^{+}\!\!\upharpoonright\!\!\mathpzc{s}}}
. By hypothesis,
\mathpzc{s}
is not a path because
\kappa^{+}
does not appear in
{\llcorner\mathpzc{s}_{1}\mathpzc{s}_{2}\lrcorner}
. But
{\llcorner\mathpzc{s}_{1}\mathpzc{s}_{2}\lrcorner}
is of the form
{\llcorner\mathpzc{s}_{1}\lrcorner}\mathpzc{s}_{2}^{\prime}
, since all the actions of
\mathpzc{s}_{2}
are hereditarily justified by the first (necessarily negative) action of
\mathpzc{s}_{2}
, indeed: we have proved that, in
\mathpzc{u}
, all the actions of
\mathpzc{u}_{2}
(in particular those of
\mathpzc{s}_{2}
) were justified in
\alpha^{-}\mathpzc{u}_{2}
. Thus
\kappa^{+}
does not appear in
{\llcorner\mathpzc{s}_{1}\lrcorner}
, which means that O-visibility is not satisfied for
\kappa^{-}
in
\mathpzc{s}^{\prime}=\mathpzc{s}_{1}\kappa^{-}\maltese
.

Hence the triple
(\mathpzc{s}^{\prime},\mathpzc{t}^{\prime},\mathpzc{u}^{\prime})
